- **battle-testing-a-skill**: Use when adversarially stress-testing whether a SKILL.md holds up under hostile or low-quality input -- probing a skill file for prompt-injection susceptibility, rubber-stamp or false-pass bias, over-broad mis-routing triggers, and missing rejection or escalation paths, especially to carry that adversarial-evaluation knowledge into a harness that lacks it built in. Distinct from untrusted-input-triage, which triages inbound external text before acting; this evaluates a skill file's own robustness. ([tvna/gitapex](https://github.com/tvna/gitapex/tree/HEAD/skills/battle-testing-a-skill))

- **bauplan-data-assessment**: Assesses whether a business question can be answered with data available in a Bauplan lakehouse. Produces a structured feasibility report. Use when a user brings a business question, asks 'can we answer this', wants to know if the data supports an analysis, or before building a one-off analysis or pipeline. - **bauplan-data-quality-checks**: Generates data quality check code for bauplan pipelines and ingestion workflows. Output is always code, never reports. ([BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills](https://github.com/BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/bauplan/skills/bauplan-data-quality-checks) · ★16)
- **bauplan-data-semantics**: Reads and drafts the semantic layer of a Bauplan lakehouse — the table-level and column-level documentation carried in Iceberg as `comment` and field `doc`. Use to find out what a column means before querying it, to diagnose a query that returned a plausible but wrong number, or to draft documentation for columns whose meaning is not obvious from name and type. Also invoked by bauplan-data-pipeline and bauplan-migrate-to-typed-sdk when a pipeline needs documentation written. Read-only: drafts documentation text, never edits model code or runs pipelines. ([BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills](https://github.com/BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/bauplan/skills/bauplan-data-semantics) · ★16)

- **bauplan-explore-data**: Explores data in a Bauplan lakehouse safely using the Bauplan Python SDK. - **baybee-diagram**: SVG diagram rendering engine for flowcharts, architecture diagrams, system illustrations, UI wireframes, and structured visuals. - **baybee-edit**: SVG scene editing engine for modifying existing SVGs without regenerating the entire scene. - **bb-invoke-conformal**: 调用 Cadence Conformal LEC (`lec`) 做逻辑等价性检查（RTL↔netlist 或 netlist↔netlist），替代开源流程缺失的 LEC 角色（netgen 只做 SPICE-LVS）。Workflow: (1) render do-file; (2) run lec -LPGXL -TCLmode -NOGui -Dofile; (3) parse Equivalent / NON-EQUIVALENT。触发场景：(1) bba-guru-synthesis post-synth 验证 RTL↔综合网表等价；(2) bba-guru-pd post-route 验证综合网表↔post-route 网表等价；(3) ECO 后证明仅改了那一处；(4) 显式 /bb-invoke-conformal。 ([essiloan/gpgpu-chiplets](https://github.com/essiloan/gpgpu-chiplets/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/bb-invoke-conformal))

- **bb-invoke-innovus**: 调用 Cadence Innovus 25.12 执行统一 Place&Route（init/floorplan/place/cts/route/opt/extract/streamout/verify_drc），取代开源 Magic+QRouter+KLayout 三件套；内建 NanoRoute + ccopt + Quantus extractRC，三角 MMMC（ss/tt/ff），产出 saveDesign .enc + routed.def + GDSII + DRC/timing 报告。Workflow: (1) render TCL; (2) run innovus -files; (3) parse DRC/timing/area。触发场景：(1) bba-guru-pd 在综合网表上跑 P&R；(2) DRC/timing fail 后迭代；(3) 显式 /bb-invoke-innovus。 ([essiloan/gpgpu-chiplets](https://github.com/essiloan/gpgpu-chiplets/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/bb-invoke-innovus))
